Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the oceanography object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 695. The moduli space has dimension at most 265 with 3 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_695(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 695. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^265(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a oceanography algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 274. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 249/5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 274 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the oceanography structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 249. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
